# Test results: Sprint 2 (US-14 to US-37)

Generated 2026-10-27 12:52 on branch `sprint4-marilyn` at commit `c1b87c7` plus uncommitted changes. It is a snapshot of one run of every suite; regenerate it after the tests change.

**How to read it.** *Expected* is every `expect(...)` check in the test's body, copied from the source. *Actual* is what happened when it ran: `Passed`, or the runner's failure message with the expected and received values. Each test is filed under the first user story it names (its comment, title, describe block or file).

Written test cases for these stories: [sprint2-test-cases.md](docs/testing/sprint2-test-cases.md).

US-14 and US-15 were planned for Sprint 1 but finished in Sprint 2, so their results are here. Their written test cases (TC-S1-103 to 111) are in [sprint1-test-cases.md](docs/testing/sprint1-test-cases.md).

Other sprints: [Sprint 1](TEST-RESULTS-SPRINT1.md) · [Sprint 3](TEST-RESULTS-SPRINT3.md) · [Sprint 4](TEST-RESULTS-SPRINT4.md) · [index](TEST-RESULTS.md)

## Summary

| Type | What it runs | Tests | Passed | Failed | Other |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Unit** | One service, rule or DTO on its own (`src/**/*.spec.ts`), database mocked. `npm test` | 985 | 985 | 0 | 0 |
| **Integration** | The real Nest app and the local `connectsphere_test` database over HTTP (`test/*.e2e-spec.ts`); also the functional tests. `npm run test:e2e` | 541 | 541 | 0 | 0 |
| **UI** | React screens with React Testing Library and Vitest (`frontend/src/**/*.test.tsx`), API mocked. `npm --prefix frontend test` | 144 | 144 | 0 | 0 |
| **E2E** | Playwright drives Chromium through the real app, API and test database (`e2e/*.spec.ts`). `npm run test:browser` | 21 | 21 | 0 | 0 |
| **Total** | | **1691** | **1691** | **0** | **0** |
